{"forms":[{"form":"leverages","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"A crowbar uses leverage to pry nails out of wood.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A force compounded by means of a lever rotating around a pivot."],"synonyms":[{"word":"mechanical advantage"}],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Try using competitors’ prices for leverage in the negotiation.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any influence which is compounded or used to gain an advantage."],"tags":["broadly","uncountable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Leverage is great until something goes wrong with your investments and you still have to pay your debts.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The use of borrowed funds with a contractually determined return to increase the ability to invest and earn an expected higher return, but usually at high risk."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["business","finance"]},{"glosses":["The debt-to-equity ratio."],"synonyms":[{"word":"gearing"}],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["business","finance"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Their variable-cost-reducing investments have dramatically increased their leverage.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The ability to earn very high returns when operating at high-capacity utilization of a facility."],"synonyms":[{"word":"operating leverage"}],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["business"]},{"glosses":["The distance of between an independent observation and other observations."],"qualifier":"regression analytics","tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["mathematics","sciences","statistics"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈlɛv.(ə.)ɹɪd͡ʒ/"},{"ipa":"/ˈliːv.(ə.)ɹɪd͡ʒ/"}],"upstream_line":300168,"upstream_offset":1498579820,"upstream_record_sha256":"af32660169961d256812fa316111f1af46c8be54a48e544adbf13e6435890230","word":"leverage"}
